- [ ] **Q2704 MEDIUM Three unit tests time out at 60 s only in the coverage run, so the Coverage floor check is red on main.** Main push run 41699549119 (f8ac218e50, 2026-11-03) and PR #4069 run 41713574349 (4f648deeaf): the merged coverage report (`npx vitest --merge-reports=coverage-blobs --coverage`) fails on "Test timed out in 60000ms" for src/test/refundsKeepTheCardFee.test.ts:1638 ("an amount decided in SQL keeps the fee ...") and src/test/mutationIsNotInert.test.ts:135 ("applying each replacement leaves no duplicate JSX prop") on both runs, and on src/test/discardedQueryFilters.test.ts ("the whole repo is clean") on main's run. Every Coverage shard job still reports pass, so only the Coverage floor job goes red. Left: make the three tests fast enough under v8 coverage (no timeout raise that hides real slowness; measure each test's time first) so the Coverage floor job is green. Not a required check (ruleset 24543186) and not in the release gate (lead 11-03). **2 OF 3 FAST ON CI; THIRD BUILT (Builder 111, 2026-11-04, ready/b111-coverage-speed):** main's Vitest run 41828763148 (6dd1f6643f, 12:22Z, after 9c89ba5a88): the merged coverage report lists ONE failure, src/test/mutationIsNotInert.test.ts:187 "Test timed out in 60000ms"; refundsKeepTheCardFee and discardedQueryFilters no longer time out. Fix: each mutation re-parses only the top-level statement it touches and walks only the elements overlapping the replaced text (same verdicts: 5,528 mutations compared against the whole-file method, 0 differences). Measured on this Mac with v8 coverage: 23.5 s -> 13.3 s (without coverage 7.3 s -> 2.8 s). Timeout unchanged at 60 s. GUARD: src/test/mutationIsNotInert.test.ts 're-parsing only the changed statement finds the same duplicate prop as the whole file' (2 @mutates killed by hand). Done when: a main Vitest run's merged coverage report lists no timeout.

- [~] **Q1655 LOW email-unsubscribe performs the write on a GET, now for job-match mail too (lh-authz-rls review of Q1585, 2026-10-12; inherited from the promotions flow).** supabase/functions/email-unsubscribe/index.ts writes before any method check, and the job-match List-Unsubscribe URL is header-only, so any client or scanner that GETs it turns off that member's new-job emails. RFC 8058 clients POST. Fix: GET renders a confirm page that POSTs; POST (One-Click) writes. Same file: the resubscribe confirmation pages (promotions and job_matches) are unreachable because every POST returns text first. finding: lh-authz-rls@57aea077b#3 **BUILT 2026-10-17 (sec-c, ready/sec-c-1017-11; live proof owed after deploy; lead decision: build the web page):** a GET now writes nothing and does no read: it answers 303 to the new public web page /unsubscribe with the same e/sig/list (src/pages/auth/Unsubscribe.tsx, on the AuthShell card). That page asks "Turn off new-job emails?" / "Unsubscribe from promotional email?" with one primary button, POSTs `action=unsubscribe` and shows the result with a Resubscribe button (`action=resubscribe`). Page POSTs answer JSON; the RFC 8058 one-click POST (any other body) still writes and answers text; signature checking is unchanged. FOUND on the way (folded in here, not filed): the old HTML pages could never render, because Supabase serves an edge function's HTML as text/plain (MEASURED 2026-10-17: GET with an invalid token answered 400 `content-type: text/plain` + nosniff with the whole <!DOCTYPE html> page as text). So a member who clicked the footer link saw raw HTML source. NOT measured: the old 200 page for a real signed link. The poster test account gets no job-match email (send-notification-email: skipped test_recipient), and the temporary signing function that would have made a link was refused deployment by the permission system. GUARDS: src/test/edge/unsubscribeGetWritesNothing.test.ts (GET valid/forged -> 303 to the page with exactly e/sig/list and zero writes; page POSTs write and answer JSON; a bad signature writes nothing; one-click unchanged; RED on the old function 6 of 7, 4/4 mutates killed) and src/test/unsubscribePage.test.tsx (the page sends nothing until the button, reads success only from the matching JSON status, offers Resubscribe; 3/3 mutates killed). Done when, on prod, a GET of a real link writes nothing and lands on /unsubscribe, and its button turns the email off. **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, src/pages/auth/Unsubscribe.tsx and src/test/edge/unsubscribeGetWritesNothing.test.ts are on origin/main; the live GET-writes-nothing proof on prod is still owed (no done-when marker).** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: On prod, prove a GET of a real unsubscribe link writes nothing and its button turns the email off.

open from them gets a line here, with the check that guards it once one exists.

(2) every item still open from older sections, one line each with its current

**Done items leave this file (Q16, 2026-09-26):** a ticked `- [x]` item is moved
verbatim to the dated archive docs/archive/OPEN-done-YYYY-MM.md by
`npm run inventories:refresh` (scripts/archive-done.mjs); check:generated fails
while one is left here. The queue score, next free number and the done-item
guard check read this file AND the archives, so archiving never changes a count.

**`[~]` items say when they are done (2026-09-27):** put what is left as one or
more markers on the item, written out in full and ANDed together:
``done-when: sql `SELECT <one column>` => <expected>`` (read-only, first row),
`done-when: test <path>`, `done-when: issue #N closed`, or `done-when: pr #N merged`.
Only add markers when they cover everything that is left (a screenshot or a
review still to do means no marker yet). `node scripts/open-done-when.mjs` runs them;
the nightly `open-done-when` workflow opens one nightly-red issue listing the items
that are ready to tick. src/test/openPartlyDoneItemsSayDoneWhen.test.ts ratchets the
count of `[~]` items with no marker.

5. **Push notifications (Q82): three things only you can do.** The code
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "cut it when main is current".** Claude runs fastlane beta from main
   once the open landings are in and main CI is green; you install it and open the app once.
   cause is fixed and pushed (see Q82), but no phone has the fix yet.
   (a) **Cut a TestFlight build from main** (at or after the Q82 fix commit)
   and install it on your iPhone. The only builds with the AppDelegate
   forwarding are ce4d38d59 (2026-09-02) and later, and every build so far
   also has the boot race (it depends on timing, so it may not hit every
   launch). (b) **On that build: sign in, go to Home, tap "Enable" on the
   notifications pill, then Allow.** The pill only shows after you've posted
   or applied, or on a later launch more than an hour after install. If you tapped "Don't
   Allow" on any earlier build, iOS will not ask again: turn it on in
   Settings > Helpr > Notifications first, then cold-launch the app. Within
   seconds `select count(*) from push_tokens` should be 1 and
   analytics_events should show `push_token_saved`. If it doesn't, the
   analytics events say which step failed. (c) **Only if you run the app
   straight from Xcode on your phone:** those builds get SANDBOX APNs tokens,
   and prod sends to the production APNs host (`APNS_USE_SANDBOX` is unset),
   so APNs rejects them with BadDeviceToken and the sender deletes the row.
   TestFlight/App Store builds are unaffected. Leave this alone unless you
   test from Xcode. APNs credentials are fine: APNS_KEY_ID, APNS_TEAM_ID
   (digest matches P85MCK558V), APNS_BUNDLE_ID (digest matches com.Helpr)
   and APNS_AUTH_KEY are all set in Supabase secrets. Whether the .p8 key
   itself is valid can't be checked without a real token to send to.

7. **Turn off Supabase's GitHub auto-deploy to production (Q121). Top priority.**
   On every push to main, Supabase's GitHub integration applies new migrations
   (and deploys edge functions) to prod within about a minute. Our deploy
   workflow's safety checks (lint, replay test, destructive-DDL stop) run after
   that, so for the last 24h (24 migrations) they have not blocked anything.
   Measured from prod's own logs 2026-09-23 (Q117). What you do: Supabase
   dashboard > Project Settings > Integrations > GitHub, and switch off
   automatic deploys to the production branch (exact toggle name not verified).
   Until then, each new migration turns db-deploy red by design (the new
   provenance check), so the side channel stays visible.
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner asked "whats the best option?"): switch it OFF.** Measured: db-deploy
   36261725456 (18:16Z) judged 124 prod versions since the cutoff, 108 with a db-deploy receipt and 16
   acknowledged side-channel applies, the newest of them 2026-09-23 ~16:10Z (Q194). So nothing has come
   in by the side channel for about 3 days, but that is not proof the toggle is off. Off is the only
   setting where db-deploy's lint, replay and destructive-DDL checks actually run before prod changes.
   Still the owner's dashboard click; the provenance check stays as the detector either way.

7. **Backups (Q45): the restore is proven weekly now; five things are yours.**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-ups): (a) yes, Pro is paid and intended; (b) keep 14-day retention; (c) back up uploaded files EXCEPT id-documents, encrypted (work item Q147); (d) no PITR (no paid upgrade); (e) BACKUP_PASSPHRASE and ban_fingerprint_salt go in the owner's password manager (owner to-do).**
   Measured 2026-09-23. Nothing below is needed for the drill to stay green.
   (a) **Is the Supabase org really on Pro?** The API says `plan: "pro"`, and
   `supabase backups list` shows 7 daily platform backups. Every doc said
   "free tier". If you are paying $25/mo, you already have in-place daily
   restores for 7 days. If you did not mean to be on Pro, that is a bill.
   (b) **Backup retention is 14 days, not 90.** The repo caps artifacts and
   logs at 14 days (`maximum_allowed_days` 90). Raising it to 90 is one
   setting, but it lengthens EVERY workflow's artifacts, and on the free
   GitHub plan that can go past the artifact-storage quota (billed). The
   alternative is a monthly copy somewhere else. Your call.
   (c) **Storage files (photos, ID documents) are in NO backup**: 152 objects,
   about 10 MB (proof-photos 64, job-photos 61, message-attachments 18,
   user-documents 4, avatars 4, id-documents 1). The platform backup excludes
   them too. Backing them up needs a service-role key as an Actions secret and
   puts ID documents (encrypted) into a GitHub artifact: a privacy trade-off.
   (d) **PITR** (restore to the minute) is a paid add-on, about $100/mo plus
   Small compute. Without it, worst-case loss is about 24 h (platform) or
   24 h + GitHub's up-to-7 h schedule delay (ours). You said no paid upgrades,
   so this is only the measured gap.
   (e) **Two secrets that no backup holds.** `BACKUP_PASSPHRASE`: is there a
   copy outside GitHub? If not, losing the GitHub account also loses every
   backup. `ban_fingerprint_salt` (vault): it cannot be re-created, and
   without it every stored ban fingerprint stops matching after a restore.
   Want it (encrypted) in the backup, or kept in your password manager?

15. **Tax forms (W-9) after a Helpr deletes their account (CS-003, 2026-09-24).**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-24 (owner pop-up): keep 4 years after signing, then delete automatically. Work item Q370.**
   Deleting an account leaves the Helpr's W-9 record (typed legal name, signing IP)
   in place forever. The IRS expects a business to keep W-9s for about 4 years, so
   deleting them at once may be wrong too. Options: (a) keep them 4 years after
   deletion, then delete automatically; (b) delete them with the account; (c) keep
   forever. I recommend (a), but it is a legal call. No W-9s exist in prod yet.

29. **PR #1810 (Q392): which parish job-match alert design stays? (2026-09-26).** PR #1810 and main
    (20260926041132, Q225/V-008) both fix "parish alert arrives before early access shows the job", differently:
    (A) KEEP MAIN'S queue-only design (every send goes through the 1-minute sweep, so elite/no-delay users hear up
    to ~60s later; nothing sent inside the funding write) and ADD the PR's block check (`user_blocks`, both ways)
    and shared `job_announceable_to()` gate. A user who deletes the alert can be told again if the job reopens.
    (B) TAKE THE PR'S design: elite/no-delay users told inline at funding (incl. email via net.http_post inside the
    escrow write), a permanent per-(user, job) `job_match_queue` ledger so nobody is ever told twice, block check;
    main's `parish_match_alert_queue` and its sweep step are retired.
    Measured live (pg_get_functiondef, prod): neither `notify_helpers_on_job_post` nor `deliver_parish_match_alert`
    checks `user_blocks` today; `deliver_job_match`/`job_announceable_to` do not exist on prod.
    Not blocked by this: the PR's instant-match half, digest gating, export_my_data line, and Q401 (auth-page
    noindex) are being landed separately. The PR's old-timestamp migrations must never reach main as-is.
    **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "A + never twice": keep main's queue-only send path, add the
    `user_blocks` check (both ways) and `job_announceable_to()` gate, AND write parish sends into the permanent
    per-(user, job) ledger so a deleted alert is never re-sent when the job reopens. Lands after the instant half.**

31. **Group crews: three money rules (Q728/Q729, 2026-09-27).** Group jobs are off
    (GROUP_JOBS_ENABLED=false), so nothing is charged under these yet. (A) CREW_FEE_PAYS_UNCONFIRMED:
    a late-cancel fee is shared by a member the poster OFFERED the job to but who never confirmed.
    Built as yes (your Q407(13) answer); confirm. (B) CREW_COMPLETES_WHEN_HIRED_DONE: an under-filled
    crew completes when every hired member is done, and the unfilled shares go back to the poster.
    Built as yes; confirm. (C) New: when a crew dispute or an unfilled slot returns a member's share,
    only the share itself (budget + urgent fee) goes back. The platform fee, sales tax and any gift
    card on that charge are NOT returned. Should they be returned in proportion to the share?
    ANSWERED 2026-09-27: yes, pro-rata. BUILT (see the ANSWERED block above); guard src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts.

## FEEDS — mirrored from the alert ledger, nightly-red issues and the audit bus (node scripts/open-sync-trackers.mjs)

Each item mirrors one open problem; its `feed:` tags are sticky (never edit them) and its `done-when:` holds once the source closes, when the sync flips it to `[~]`. The same problem seen again while its item is open is attached to that item (marked Seen again), never filed twice; every red overnight check is one sub-line of the ONE Fix the test robots item, which the sync rewrites.

- [~] **Q865 MEDIUM** (nightly-red #1754 prod-audit, 2026-09-30): 156 of run 36766443019's 158 skips are NOT justified. deep-links, messy-input and interruptions call `ensureFundedOpenJob` in `beforeAll`; under live Stripe it calls `skipLivePay` (`test.skip`), which in a `beforeAll` skips EVERY test in the file, including those that never touch the funded job (signed-out redirects, gone-job, /user/:id, the six /profile?tab= links). Only tests that read `fx.openJob` should skip; they already carry per-test `test.skip(!fx.openJob, …)`. The other 2 skips (expanding-search) are justified. Fix: let the fixture return "none" in live mode instead of skipping, keep the live-pay skip per test, and GUARD the class: a `beforeAll` must not call a fixture that can `test.skip` (static scan of e2e/prod-audit, @mutate one spec back). Run 36364639347 (before the switch, 124 tests) measured 109.9, which is inside the two-way band, so the un-skip will not trip it; lower it to the new measurement in the same commit. done-when: issue #2245 closed (tick when a prod-audit run shows only justified skips, and `prod-audit.perTest` equals its measured value). **Fixed 2026-09-30 (Q865 lane):** the three beforeAll hooks now mint inside `unlessLivePay(() => ...)` (e2e/prod-audit/fundedOpenJob.ts), which turns the live-pay skip into a value; only tests that open a funded fixture skip, per test, via `skipWhenUnfundedLive` -> `skipLivePay`. Of the 156 (`playwright test --list`): deep-links 18 of 21 un-skipped, interruptions 5 of 12 (the 7 apply tests still skip), messy-input 112 of 123 (4 applicant sweeps, 2 pending-applicant sweeps and 5 openJob/pending/disputed explores still skip); 135 un-skipped, 21 skip with the live-pay reason. - [~] **Q2410 MEDIUM /messages · undefined is not an object (evaluating 'e._result.default') (2026-10-25).** From alert-ledger rows 5a28db44e912 (user-error-screen, /messages, error_logs 2328bb32, 2026-10-25 22:13Z) and c5d2aafd1f77 (Sentry JAVASCRIPT-4J, /login, 2026-10-23, same TypeError). feed: ledger 5a28db44e912. feed: ledger c5d2aafd1f77. **ROOT CAUSE MEASURED 2026-10-27:** the /messages row's release 3caabd851f was mid prod-deploy (prod-deploy run 22:11:06-22:13:44Z); its component stack is Lazy > Suspense > SidePanelProvider, i.e. one of the seven shell lazies in App.tsx's nav Suspense, caught by the top-level ErrorBoundary. Vite 8.3.4's preload helper (read in node_modules) resolves a failed import() with undefined once main.tsx's vite:preloadError handler calls preventDefault() to start a recovery; React.lazy caches that and reads .default off it on every later render. The boundaries stayed quiet only while the recovery flag was up, so the same dead lazy surfacing after the flag dropped (a scheduled retry cancelled by navigating, or declined at fire time) was the generic card, a report, and a Try Again that re-threw. **FIXED (ready/lazy-poisoned-chunk-1027):** isChunkLoadError matches the poisoned-lazy read (WebKit, V8, Gecko, React-dev wordings), so every boundary recovers with a bounded reload or, when recovery is over, shows the honest card. Left: deploy, then close the two ledger rows. done-when: test src/test/errorBoundariesQuietDuringChunkRecovery.test.tsx **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Close ledger rows 5a28db44e912 and c5d2aafd1f77.
- [~] **Q2498 MEDIUM schedule-stalled: 1 scheduled workflow(s) have stopped running.** Mirrored 2026-10-31 from schedule-stalled issue #3823 and alert-ledger row 8a2e802edbe7 by `scripts/open-sync-trackers.mjs`: find the root cause, fix it, re-run the source's own detector. feed: issue #3823 · feed: ledger 8a2e802edbe7. done-when: issue #3823 closed, done-when: sql `SELECT status FROM public.ops_alert_ledger WHERE fingerprint = '8a2e802edbe7bd772cfe6fec1ea6488b'` => closed **ROOT CAUSE 2026-11-02 (Builder 9, measured via gh api; evidence ~/.lh-shots/q2498/evidence.txt):** the repo's Actions artifact-and-log retention is 2 days (`actions/permissions/artifact-and-log-retention` -> days 2), and run RECORDS vanish with it: runs created 2026-10-31 22:20:08Z answered 200 at 22:05Z today and 404 at 22:24Z (~48 h old); no run of any event exists for 2026-10-31 00:00-22:00Z. schedule-heartbeat reads `runs?event=schedule&per_page=1`, so a weekly workflow's run is gone 2 days after it fires: supabase-usage.yml and write-contract-refresh.yml (Saturday crons, budget 8 d) show their last surviving run, 2026-10-24, and every workflow with a budget over 2 days follows. Not a broken workflow. Fix: retention back to at least 14 days (the longest heartbeat budget, and migration-provenance.json's minAgeDays 14, whose lostRuns list grows for the same reason). That is a repo setting the owner chose, so it goes to the lead; no code change. **FIX 2026-11-02 ~22:50Z (lead):** the lead set the retention to 30 days; Builder 9 read it back at 22:55Z: `gh api repos/louisianahelpr/louisianahelpr/actions/permissions/artifact-and-log-retention` -> {"days":30}. Runs from before the change still expire on their old 2-day clock. Done when schedule-heartbeat sees a weekly scheduled run older than 2 days (supabase-usage.yml and write-contract-refresh.yml fire Saturday 2026-11-07; their runs must still be listed on 11-10), the heartbeat run is green and issue #3823 closes.

(archive L2133) **OWNER DECIDED 2026-10-04 (pop-up, with Q1076): tests get ONE permanent test-only paid is_seed job set in the database (no real money, never shown to real users, excluded from payout jobs).** **SCOPED 2026-10-05 (alerts lane): this build is now the root cause of nightly-red #2245 (prod-audit, 21 of 25 failures), #2213 and #2294 (Q722 empty-account shapes), part of #2198, and so #2244.** The states the suites need between poster-e2e and helper-e2e: an open escrow job the Helpr has not applied to; accepted and confirmed but not on the way; on the way / in_progress; completed with no review; completed with a review the Helpr can answer; an unanswered dispute against the Helpr. The hard part is money: is_seed alone does not keep these rows away from auto-release-payment, process-scheduled-payouts, void-cancelled-payments, auto-resolve-disputes (a durable dispute would be auto-resolved and try a Stripe refund), charge-recurring-visits, auto-expire-jobs and money-reconciliation. That needs a dedicated fixture marker that every money cron and the reconciliation skip, a guard that derives the money crons and requires the skip, and an lh-money-escrow review. It is too large to start at the end of this session. Next: build it as one money-reviewed lane. **MERGED IN 2026-10-06 (triage):** Q941 (the 7 interruptions.spec.ts apply tests skip until the open un-applied escrow job exists; prod-audit.yml runs no seeder; still 7 skips on run 37270491218) and Q1076 (seed producers bypass accept_job/respond_to_direct_offer and leave states real jobs never reach; per the same 2026-10-04 decision they stamp states realistically). **MERGED IN 2026-10-06 (triage, second pass):** Q856 (shell-spacing reports messages@320-430 UNMEASURED until poster-e2e has two Active threads on live jobs). Q593 (a11y-prod's RESERVED accepted fixture) is the same build but stays its own item: it now mirrors nightly-red #2375. **DESIGN 2026-10-06 (e2e-reds lane; owner re-confirmed ONE permanent paid is_seed job, never public; lead: post-launch, both reviews before landing):** measured on prod 2026-10-06: anon open_jobs_browse = [] and 0 open/accepted/in_progress jobs with payment_status escrow (119 open abandoned seeds, 1 open unpaid, 1 accepted unpaid), so a11y-prod job-detail-1 skips UNJUSTIFIED (6 in run 37403676136) and prod-audit 37521973791 fails 16 tests + 7 unjustified skips on 'no in-progress job between the shared accounts'. (a) VISIBILITY: seed_jobs_hidden_publicly() is false on prod (feature_flags key unset), so an escrow seed would show on public Browse today (prod-seed.mjs:534 refuses escrow seeds for this reason), and flipping it true would also hide the row from helper-e2e. Migration: seed jobs visible only to viewers whose profiles.is_seed is true, on all four browse surfaces (open_jobs_browse, get_ranked_open_jobs, get_open_jobs_for_map, get_public_open_jobs): lh-authz-rls review. (b) UNHIREABLE: an escrow row with no PaymentIntent that press-every-control or a journey could apply to, hire and release would make release-payout transfer from the LIVE platform balance (no source_transaction). A fixture marker that apply_to_job / accept_application / release paths and every money cron listed above refuse, plus a guard deriving the money crons: lh-money-escrow review. (c) a service-role ensure script run by each workflow's fixtures job; a11y-prod authedScreens and the prod-audit gap lookups read the fixture id instead of open_jobs_browse. **BUILT 2026-10-07 (proof lane, owner decision 2026-10-07: ONE durable is_seed job funded in the database only, visible only to the test accounts, applied to and withdrawn from by 01-browse):** migration 20261007122020 adds public.test_fixture_jobs (server-only) and trg_jobs_test_fixture_never_hired, which for a listed job refuses, for every role, a Helpr, a direct offer, any status but open/cancelled and a payment_status move while open (PGlite src/test/pglite/testFixtureJobsCannotBeHired.pglite.mjs: 12 FAILED without it, ALL PASS with it, 3x replay); scripts/e2e/browseFixture.mjs keeps exactly one (ends an unusable open one before unregistering it); e2e/journeys/01-browse.spec.ts ensures it, applies, withdraws and cleans up in a finally. Guard: src/test/testFixtureJobsCannotBeHired.test.ts (6 @mutate, each red). lh-money-escrow review: SAFE WITH FIXES, both closed: (1) an unusable open fixture is ended before it is unregistered; (2) CI's poster is poster-e2e (71c56dfb), measured read-only: all 28 '[E2E DO NOT ACCEPT]' jobs created inside e2e-journeys 37552757280 + 37554735234's windows (00:35-01:47Z) and all 42 since 00:34Z are owned by 71c56dfb, none by 96c9899e; locally getSession('poster') mints poster-e2e too, so CI and local keep the same fixture. Still open in this item: the other suite states (accepted, in progress, completed, dispute: prod-audit / a11y / press), the /jobs:id + /user:id stall, press self-heal and the push 'Not Now' signal. **STATE FIXTURES BUILT 2026-10-12 (lane seed-fixtures, branch ready/seed-fixtures), applied to prod the same day:** scripts/e2e/seedStateFixtures.mjs (`--apply` idempotent, `--verify` read-only, `--teardown`, `--only=<key>`) keeps these poster-e2e jobs, is_seed, funded in the database only (escrow, no PaymentIntent, no Checkout Session), helper-e2e hired through the app's own RPCs: helper-scheduled-4d a87934d0-bb91-5215-b509-d49744cb2c5a and helper-scheduled-daybefore 9cd3fa38-7e53-57ca-813b-71bc15c8e389 (accepted; each with a 2-message thread; for Q1554, Q1561, Q593, Q1427), helper-arrived-photo 90aa6c8b-bf01-5430-ab29-ac182087ad5e (photo required, GPS-verified arrival, no before photo; Q1562), helper-completed c975aec8-ef5a-5fca-bac1-237320e7e557 (completed, payment_status released in the DB only; Q1561, work record), dispute-refiled a690c95f-ecd8-5791-86ce-b21b0c797674 (disputed, Helpr answered, withdrawn, re-filed; Q1327), crew-unconfirmed 0f2f7753-9390-57d1-8c4c-fcc84259bad3 (booked crew of helper-e2e + eli, neither confirmed; Q1402), and wipe row 2edc2b13-de6b-5d52-9528-c5fc168e4d81 in pre_verification_wipes for the incomplete account 60416646 (storage_done_at set, so the wipe sweep skips it; Q1286). NOT listed in test_fixture_jobs (its trigger refuses every status move, and a refusal inside a cron's set-based sweep would roll the sweep back for real users); the money safety is that every money mover refuses a job with no PaymentIntent before calling Stripe, the money crons skip is_seed, and no test account has a Stripe account. prod-seed.mjs never reviews or disputes them. - [ ] **Q654 MEDIUM Take ~140 KiB of unused JS off the boot path (Q56 rank 3).** Lighthouse unused-javascript 138-146 KiB on every public route; boot graph 294 KB gz. Lower scripts/perf/critical-path-budget.json with the change. **FIXED IN PART 2026-10-04 (cloud/perf-first-paint, DRAFT PR; protection pending live measurement): about 26 KB gz of the boot path, NOT the whole ~140 KiB.** Cause found: vite.config.ts's `app-shared` group (boot path of every page) captured every src/lib|hooks|... module two chunks share, and ~160 modules only SIGNED-IN pages import (useActivityData, awardGate, ...) rode in it. scripts/perf/bootReach.mjs computes per build which shared modules no signed-out cold load reaches (static imports from main.tsx and each guest first screen in ENTRY_ROUTE_CHUNKS) and vite.config.ts gives them a `signed-in-shared` group (`includeDependenciesRecursively: false`: with the default rolldown also pulls utils, the Supabase client and react-router into it, boot imports it, and nothing moves, measured 280 KB). Measured with npm run build, origin/main c68ac5f -> this branch, gzip: boot graph 279.0 -> 252.8 KB (raw 896 -> 818 KiB; app-shared 118.5 -> 92.2 KB + signed-in-shared 27.6 KB off the boot path); critical-path routes / 295 -> 269, /browse 364 -> 337, /login 297 -> 271, /signup 331 -> 305, /posts 324 -> 325 (the same signed-in code in two chunks); scripts/perf/critical-path-budget.json lowered to those. Chunk graph has 0 static cycles before and after; public routes load with no page or console errors in Chromium (/, /login, /signup, /browse, /home -> /login, /legal). GUARDS: check-deferred-vendors.mjs "signed-in-only shared module is on the boot path" reads the built sourcemaps against bootReach.mjs (RED on the origin/main build: app-shared carries 90 such modules; red again with the group removed in vite.config.ts, rebuilt: boot back to 279 KB), src/test/bootReach.test.ts (@mutate x4, all red), criticalPathBudget (exact both ways). What is LEFT of the ~140 KiB, measured with Chromium JS coverage on a cold `/` (gz-weighted unused, local build, no backend): supabase-js 54 KB gz 89% unused (auth, storage, realtime clients all built at startup; not split here), framer proxy.js 39 KB gz 90% unused fetched right after load on every route by App.tsx's lazy PageTransition and ScrollToTop (Q1299), react-router/react-dom/tanstack ~60 KB gz of which about half unused (library cost), and a second tier of ~15 KB gz only /browse needs (useDashboardFilters, useMapKitJs, smartSort...) still in app-shared. Lighthouse itself was not run here (lead). Real before/after Lighthouse unused-javascript still to measure. **RECHECK 2026-10-12 (proof4 lane):** NOT done. Missing: the real before/after Lighthouse unused-javascript measurement (a browser run; not done in this lane) and the remaining ~110 KiB (supabase-js, framer proxy, /browse-only modules) still listed above. **REOPENED 2026-10-13 (proof2 lane): not a proof item.** About 26 KB gz of the ~140 KiB came off; the rest (supabase-js ~54 KB gz, framer proxy ~39 KB gz on every route via PageTransition/ScrollToTop (Q1299), /browse-only modules ~15 KB gz) is unbuilt, and vite.config.ts and the budget are unchanged since 2026-10-11. The Lighthouse before/after belongs with that change. **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, only ~26 KB of the ~140 KiB was taken off; the rest and the budget lowering are not done.** **BUILT 2026-11-03 (Builder 40, wip/b40-1103), part only:** App.tsx mounts ScrollToTop and PermissionRationaleMount for guests on the first frame, but bootReach counted only main.tsx and the guest pages as public reach, so their helpers (hideUnopenedInApp, lazyModule) were filed signed-in-only and those two lazy chunks pulled signed-in-shared (14.6 KB gz, 85% unused), signed-in-dock (4.6) and browse-home-shared onto every public boot (~140 ms, before load). bootReach.mjs GUEST_CHROME_ROOTS now counts them as public reach. Measured, local build (bundle-size.yml env), Chromium JS coverage, 1440, cold, no backend, 6 s window, gz-weighted unused JS, origin/main dca60fb752 -> this branch: / 255.7 -> 251.9 KB, /login 218.0 -> 201.5, /signup 212.7 -> 196.2, /browse 223.8 -> 207.4. critical-path.mjs --check green, budget unchanged (route closures do not count the lazy chrome). Guards: src/test/bootReach.test.ts (GUEST_CHROME_ROOTS equals App.tsx's guest lazy chrome; nothing it reaches is signed-in-only; red with the fix reverted) and check-deferred-vendors.mjs (guest chrome closures carry no signed-in-only module; red on the main build, 3 violations). Still left of the ~140 KiB: supabase-js (24 of 29 KB gz unused), app-shared (41 of 72 unused: react-router, tailwind-merge, Capacitor core, Home's shared money helpers kept there for /home LCP), and the idle prefetches the landing starts after load (Legal's sections, owner 09-11 warm; they import signed-in-shared statically) - Lighthouse itself not run (not installed). **BUILT 2026-11-03 (Builder 41, wip/b41-1103, part):** declutter-item-pages no longer rides the job lists' first round: bootReach.mjs declutterItemSplit itemShared (the item-page modules the rest of signed-in-pages reaches) gets its own item-shared group. Built static closures, gzip: /jobs 465.7 -> 453.8 KB, Declutter item 428.4 -> 428.8, Messages 504.6 -> 505.1, /home and /profile unchanged; entry +25 B; critical-path --check green (bundle-size.yml env), budget unchanged. supabase-js: measured not split here (realtime, storage, passkey and admin are already stubbed; what is left is GoTrueClient itself). Legal idle prefetch left alone (owner 09-11).

Now that assembly/handyman are taxed (Q1578), a poster billed in another parish pays that parish's rate, and one billed out of state pays $0 with no alert (taxedZeroOnTaxableLouisianaLabor only fires for LA billing). Fix: give Stripe the job site as the tax address (Stripe prefers a shipping address), so preview == charge. But writing the job site onto the shared Stripe Customer persists: Subscriptions and invoices also tax `customer.shipping` first, so a Pro/Plus subscription (txcd_10103001, taxable SaaS) and every renewal would be taxed at the last job site, and a ZIP outside Louisiana stamped as state LA may make Stripe refuse every automatic-tax checkout on that customer (customer_tax_location_invalid; unmeasured). Options for the next build: (a) Checkout without automatic_tax, with a precomputed "Sales tax" line from a tax.calculations call at the job site, committed with createFromCalculation on completion (what recurring visits already do); or (b) shipping_address_collection so the poster confirms the job address in Checkout. Either way, validate the ZIP is 700xx-714xx and send the same address shape to the preview (Q1657). **TRIED AGAIN 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-4), dropped before landing:** the build saved the job ZIP as customer.shipping before the session, i.e. the unsafe first design above; the lh-money-escrow review blocked it on the same grounds (create-pro-checkout taxes the same Customer with automatic_tax, so a Pro/Plus checkout and every renewal would carry the last job site; two overlapping checkouts can tax one job at the other's site). The next build is option (a) or (b) above. **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, the tax address choice (a) or (b) is not built.** **CHECKED 2026-11-03 6:07 PM (Builder 81):** not started. It overlaps in-flight tax work: wip/b71-tax and wip/b77-legacy-tax-cap both change supabase/functions/create-payment/index.ts (+144/-22 lines between them) and calculate-tax/index.ts. Both designs on file also need Stripe test-mode measurements (tax.calculations, or shipping_address_collection), which this lane may not run. Pick it up after those branches land.

- [ ] **Q2717 MEDIUM The gift-reduced tax reversal is proportional and over-reports about 39c to a few dollars per cancelled gift job (re-review of 5d8ac2ebf8, 2026-11-03).** finding: lead-rereview@5d8ac2ebf8#1 reverseVisitTaxForRefund scales a refund on a gift-reduced job-site charge by tx/card, so after a cancel that keeps fees the remaining reported labor + tax is tx x (1 - R/card), more than the tax on what was actually kept. Both crew callers now keep tax at the job's rate: void-cancelled-payments' crew fee share (taxableCents from pi.metadata) and process-scheduled-payouts' crewTaxKeptAt (jobTaxableCents read off the PaymentIntent in Step 3). Proof: stripe-webhook.test.ts Q2717 ($20 card on a $110 transaction, $16.15 refund with $9.65 tax-back: reversal 10615, leaving $3.85 = $3.50 kept + 35c; old 8883) + unstamped control; gift-card-fee Q2718 cases assert the stamps; create-payment.test.ts Q2717 stamp; process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts Q2717 (3626c, old 3621c); void-cancelled-payments-crew-deleted-member.test.ts Q2717 (5250c, old 5500c). Each red on the old source; 7 @mutates killed by hand. **REVIEW FIX (lh-money-escrow on fa77e8fed2 blocked one case):** a later UNSTAMPED refund after a stamped cancel (an admin partial refund) was scaled by txTotal / card and over-reversed (a 5250c crew share reversed 10023c, $2.27 of tax under-reported). Now, on a charge with any stamped refund, an unstamped refund reverses only its own amount; the crew fee share refund stamps its own tax-back (keptTaxCents); stamped reversals round down. Proof: stripe-webhook.test.ts "Q2717 review" (47250 + 5250, reported 10500 >= 10000 kept + 500 tax), red on fa77e8fed2; crew share stamp asserted; 2 @mutates killed by hand. - [~] **Q1737 MEDIUM A missing /assets/ or /fonts/ file now 404s, but the 404 carries `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` (found proving Q1720, 2026-10-12).** MEASURED with curl -sI on www (before 21:33Z, build c4b1b5254): /assets/missing-Q1663-x.js, /assets/notificationFeed-B4je1ZJb.js and /fonts/missing-x.woff2 all answer 404 text/plain with that header, because vercel.json's `/assets/(.*)` and `/fonts/(.*)` header rules stamp every response under those paths, found or not. A browser may keep a 404 with explicit freshness, so a chunk asked for in a deploy's propagation window (Q199) could stay a cached 404 under that URL. Not reproduced in a browser. Fix to weigh: limit the immutable header to files that exist (e.g. `missing` on the header rule, or a 404 page with no-store). GUARD when built: extend src/test/immutablePathsNeverServeTheSpa.test.ts, plus a live curl in a post-deploy check. **BUILT 2026-10-17 (sec-c, ready/sec-c-1017-10; live proof owed after deploy):** vercel.json rewrites `/assets/(.*)` and `/fonts/(.*)` to the new api/missing-asset.ts (404 text/plain, `Cache-Control: no-store`). A rewrite never shadows a real file (filesystem first), and the function's own header beats the vercel.json rule: MEASURED on a throwaway Vercel PREVIEW (louisianahelpr-ny0sdqpp9, deleted after) carrying this exact headers/rewrites block, with `vercel curl -sI` and GET: /assets/real-Q1737.js and /fonts/real-Q1737.woff2 200 `public, max-age=31536000, immutable` (second GET x-vercel-cache HIT, still immutable); /assets/missing-Q1737-x.js, /assets/sub/dir/missing.css and /fonts/missing-x.woff2 404 `no-store`; /home 200 the SPA. Prod before (2026-10-17 ~10:00Z, scripts/check-missing-asset-headers.mjs against www): the three missing paths 404 with the immutable header, rc=1. GUARDS: src/test/immutablePathsNeverServeTheSpa.test.ts (every immutable prefix's missing files reach the no-store function first; the function's real 404; the live check's verdict red on the measured prod row and green on the measured preview, fail-closed on an empty read; 7/7 mutates killed) and a live step in prod-freshness.yml's served-commit job (scripts/check-missing-asset-headers.mjs, after prod serves the commit; it also requires a real hashed asset to stay immutable). - [~] **Q1876 MEDIUM Gift card is consumed at ACH checkout completion but not restored if the ACH debit bounces (ME-046 follow-up, Sentry bot finding on PR #3176, 2026-10-16).** `checkoutSessionCompleted` marks the gift card "redeemed" before detecting `isAsyncPayment`. If the ACH debit later bounces, `paymentIntentPaymentFailed` sets the job to "failed" but never calls `restore_gift_card_for_job`, leaving the donor's credit permanently spent on a job that was never funded. Only affects jobs that used a gift card for partial payment AND paid via ACH (US bank account). Fix: in `paymentIntentPaymentFailed`, when the job transitions to "failed" and `payment_method_types` includes "us_bank_account" (or `isAsyncPayment` is detectable), call `restore_gift_card_for_job` if a gift card was applied to the job. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook test case with a gift-card-applied job and a `payment_intent.payment_failed` event that asserts the gift card is restored. **BUILT 2026-10-17 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-1017-2):** prod read-only first: 23 gift_cards, 0 redeemed, 0 redeemed on an unfunded job, so no recipient has lost a gift this way yet. Not restore_gift_card_for_job (per lh-money-escrow on 6b06413c7: its minted replacement is unique per job, so a failed job re-funded with it could never return the gift again, and the old 'redeemed' row stayed counted as gift escrow): new service-role RPC return_bounced_gift_card (migration 20261017074500) puts the SAME row back ('sent', no job, not redeemed, 90-day expiry floor) under the job's lock, only while the job's payment_status is 'failed', it has no open checkout and no payout_transfers row; a second call answers not_consumed; it returns the gift's payment_status so a gift whose donation was refunded is not called usable. paymentIntentPaymentFailed calls it only for pi.metadata.gift_card_id (create-payment's shortfall PaymentIntent), on the first delivery and on the retry after a throw (which now sends the notice the first delivery lost); a refusal pages money_at_risk; PGRST202 pages and lets the bounce stand. checkoutSessionCompleted's Q1417 re-claim now needs the job's CURRENT session, so a redelivered completion of the bounced checkout cannot spend the returned gift again. GUARD: src/test/edge/stripe-webhook.test.ts "Q1876 a bounced bank debit gives back the gift card" (10 new cases; 9 red on main's handlers, green after, 172/172; 8 @mutate killed) + PGlite ~/.lh-pglite/q1876.mjs (applied 3x, 20 checks, 6 planted mutants killed). lh-money-escrow on 2cd90f553: approve with nits (nits 3-6 fixed in the branch; points 1 and 2 filed as Q1985 and Q1986). [~] until the deployed webhook + function are read back live. **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, built; the after-deploy read-back of the webhook and function is not recorded (no marker).** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Read back the deployed webhook and function live, then tick.

- [ ] **Q1918 LOW A lost chargeback on a Checkout-taxed charge (a one-off job from create-payment, or a recurring visit paid through settleRecurringVisitCheckout) may leave its sales tax reported as collected (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1724 fix, 2026-10-14; not checked against Stripe).** Q1724 reverses the Tax API transaction an off-session visit charge records itself; charges taxed by Checkout `automatic_tax` are not handled on charge.dispute.closed (lost). Unverified whether Stripe Tax reverses a Checkout-taxed charge's tax on its own when a dispute is lost (its docs say it does not for the PaymentIntent-linked Tax API flow). Fix when confirmed: check in Stripe test mode or the Stripe Tax docs; if not automatic, reverse it on the lost branch of chargeDisputeClosed the way the visit Tax API reversal there does. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case with a lost dispute on a Checkout-taxed charge. finding: lh-money-escrow@803a6c258#3 (review of a superseded duplicate build of Q1724, bundle ~/.lh-backups/sec-a3-superseded-dupes-2026-10-15.bundle; main's Q1724 also reverses only the Tax API transaction a visit charge records itself) **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, not built and not checked against Stripe.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **MEASURED 2026-11-03 (Builder 69, Stripe docs, not a test-mode run):** docs.stripe.com/tax/reports, section "Tax calculations recorded in reports", lists among operations NOT reflected in Tax reports: "Disputes that are upheld by the cardholder's bank. Stripe Tax doesn't decrease the balance of the collected total tax." So a lost dispute on a Checkout-taxed charge stays reported as tax collected: the unknown is answered, the fix is needed. Not yet known: how to decrease it for a Checkout-created tax transaction (the docs' decrease operations for Checkout are a refund of the charge or a credit note; create_reversal is documented for Tax API transactions). Next: read docs.stripe.com/tax/standalone-tax-api reversal limits, then build the lost-dispute branch in chargeDisputeClosed with an edge test. (docs.stripe.com/api/tax/transactions/create_reversal, read 11-03, takes any original_transaction id and does not say whether a Checkout-created one qualifies: one Stripe TEST-mode call settles it.)
- [ ] **Q1902 LOW Three loose ends of the Q1491 job payout hold (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1491 rebuild 7133adc73, 2026-10-15).** (a) The hold is upserted before the escrow write's funded-job guard, so a delivery that turns out to be a duplicate funding (refunded in full by that branch) leaves a hold on a properly funded job: fail-safe, but it blocks a legitimate payout and the "underfunded" page is wrong. Fix: upsert with `.select("job_id")` (a row only when this delivery inserted it) and delete that hold in the duplicate-funding branch. (b) money-reconciliation excludes only Helpr holds, so a job-held payout_pending job is reported as payout_pending_stranded: load job_payout_holds and report it as held. (c) process-scheduled-payouts finds a job hold only at the claim, so every run repeats the account and transfer-group reads and rolls back the onboarding fee for each held job: call checkJobPayoutHold beside the Helpr check and skip with PAYOUT_HELD_CODE. Also weigh: void-cancelled-payments' two fee transfers send unlinked (no source_transaction) when latest_charge is missing; the trigger DETAIL says a held job pays nobody. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case of a duplicate-funded delivery (no hold left), a money-reconciliation case (held, not stranded) and a process-scheduled-payouts case (no Stripe read for a held job). finding: lh-money-escrow@7133adc73#3 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **BUILT 2026-11-03 (Builder 82, wip/b82, not landed):** (a) the duplicate-funding branch of checkoutSessionCompleted lifts a job_payout_holds row only when its reason names THIS session (`Q1491: gift card difference payment <session> `), deleted by job_id + exact reason; a hold for another session or reason stays; a failed read or delete throws so Stripe redelivers (the refund is keyed). Chosen over the `.select("job_id")` insert flag because a redelivery after a first delivery that placed the hold and then threw would not see the flag. (b) money-reconciliation loads job_payout_holds for payout_pending jobs (loadJobPayoutHolds) and reports them in payout_pending_held with job_hold: true, not payout_pending_stranded; an unreadable table exempts nothing and degrades the run. (c) process-scheduled-payouts calls checkJobPayoutHold beside the Helpr hold, before the ID check, any Stripe read or the claim, and skips with PAYOUT_HELD_CODE (unreadable = defect). Not done: the "also weigh" notes (void-cancelled-payments unlinked fee transfers; the trigger DETAIL wording). Guards: src/test/edge/stripe-webhook.test.ts "Q1902" (3 cases + lift failure), src/test/edge/payoutHold.test.ts "Q1902" (process-scheduled-payouts: no Stripe read for a held job; money-reconciliation: held, not stranded).
- [ ] **Q1912 LOW Prove in Stripe TEST mode that a FULL visit tax reversal can be undone and the visit reversed again (lh-money-escrow + lh-silent-failure reviews of Q1782 (2), 2026-10-15).** Q1782 (2) reverses a refund that returns the whole labor + tax with mode full (the lead's rule), and a refund that later fails undoes its reversal with a full reversal of that reversal transaction. Stripe documents undoing a PARTIAL reversal this way, not a full one, and nothing says a re-issued refund's own reversal is then accepted on the original (it may be refused as an over-reversal: that pages, so it is not silent, but the tax on money really returned would stay reported). Also capture Stripe's real error for a reused reversal reference (type, code, param, message) so referenceTaken() in stripe-webhook/handlers/_visitTaxReversal.ts matches it exactly, and the real idempotency-in-use error. Not done in the build: no Stripe test key is on this Mac and the live account must not get test tax transactions. Done when: one test-mode run (tax calculation, createFromCalculation, full reversal, full reversal of that reversal, a second reversal on the original) is recorded with its ids, and the test fixtures use the captured errors (or the code switches to partial flat_amount everywhere if Stripe refuses the cycle). finding: lh-money-escrow@994f077b2#1 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no test-mode run of the full-reversal cycle is recorded; the one commit naming it (e2b8f7d535) is a neighbouring tax fix, not this proof.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**

- [~] **Q2112 HIGH The test robots wrote the owner's sign-up funnel: their analytics events counted as people (found by the lead measuring prod analytics_events, 2026-10-15; not a review finding).** MEASURED 2026-10-15 ~02:40Z (prod, last 30 days, event: total / seed profile / local-preview referrer / left): signup_started 4573 / 17 / 9 / 4547 (all 4547 anonymous; 137 with a referrer); payment_made 2284 / 329 / 0 / 1955 (1938 anonymous); first_payment_collected 929 / 262 / 0 / 667; job_posted 300 / 32 / 0 / 268; signup_completed 28 / 0 / 0 / 28. Root cause: every robot (nightly journeys, press-every-control, loading-states, prod audits, the local agent harnesses) runs the real app against prod, and track() (src/lib/analytics.ts) sent their events as people and fanned them out to PostHog; only Sentry replay (Q275/Q379) and page_load timing skipped automated browsers. Anonymous past rows carry no origin or user agent, and a deleted test account's rows are anonymised (user_id null), so the old anonymous rows cannot be split into robot and person: they are left unmarked, not guessed. THE REAL FUNNEL, from the accounts themselves (auth.users joined to profiles, is_seed false, last 30 days): 33 sign-ups, all since 2026-10-09 (gmail 16, yahoo 10, icloud 3, aol 2, Apple relay 1, outlook 1), 26 of them email-confirmed; 18 people applied to a job (25 applications); 3 people posted 12 jobs, 11 of them paid. FIX (branch ready/sec-c-analytics): (1) client: src/lib/automatedBrowser.ts automationReason() (navigator.webdriver; localStorage lh_automation = "1" for a harness attached over CDP, where webdriver reads false; a test-profile session after the boot path's is_seed read) tags each row properties.automated = true + properties.automation = <reason>, set after the caller's props so it cannot be cleared; (2) PostHog: initPostHog() never starts for a robot or a dev/preview build on http://localhost (isLocalDevOrigin), and a test profile signing in opts out (optOutTestProfile, undone by resetUser on sign-out); (3) database, migration 20261015022106_analytics_tag_automated: BEFORE INSERT trigger tag_automated_analytics_event tags seed users, a local Origin header (http://localhost / 127.0.0.1, which page code cannot change; the apps are capacitor://localhost and https://localhost) and headless user agents, so an old build or a harness that forgot still lands tagged; view analytics_events_real (service role only) is the funnel without tagged or seed rows; a one-time backfill MARKS (never deletes) past seed-profile rows and local-preview-referrer rows. Guards: src/test/analyticsTagsRobotTraffic.test.ts (8 @mutate lines, all KILLED) and the PGlite proof src/test/pglite/analyticsTagAutomated.pglite.mjs (ALL PASS 21, applied 3x; NEW_MIGRATION=skip 8 FAIL). Left open: the CDP harness in ~/.lh-e2e-shared (outside the repo) should set lh_automation before load; scripts/perf/inp-lab.mjs deliberately reports webdriver false (its rows are tagged only by Origin when it runs on a preview). Found while measuring, not fixed here (filed separately if wanted): payment_made fires on any /payment-success visit, 1892 of the rows have no job_id (src/pages/post-job/PaymentSuccess.tsx no-reference branch); first_payment_collected fires when the paid-job count is 0 (`<= 1`). DONE WHEN: the migration is live (pg_get_functiondef of tag_automated_analytics_event, the trigger on analytics_events, analytics_events_real readable by service_role only in pg_class.relacl), and a day of new rows shows the robots' events tagged (select properties->>'automation', count(*) from analytics_events where created_at > <deploy> group by 1). **2026-10-21 (Builder 1i, lead's follow-up; prod read-only SQL, 7 days):** the 10-18 tagging is live (first live tag 2026-10-18 07:27Z). signup_completed is NOT a robot leak: 11 rows, 0 tagged, all anonymous (no session at complete-signup), referrers facebook.com / lm.facebook.com / louisianahelpr.com, against 13 real non-seed accounts created (auth.users join profiles, is_seed false); robots never use the sign-up form (they create accounts through the admin API: e2e/journeys/throwaway.ts, scripts/lib/privacyJourney.mjs, scripts/audit/prod-seed.mjs), and track() already tags SignupCompleted for webdriver / lh_automation / seed sessions. Deletion keeps the tag (it lives in properties; scrub_analytics_actor_from_properties strips only actor-valued keys); seed rows untagged: 0 of 1655; PGlite proof extended (4 new checks, red without the migration). The admin funnel and KPI sign-ups read profiles (is_seed false), not analytics_events; no DB function or edge function counts analytics_events for people. The remaining leak was the analytics-freshness monitor: job_posted counted 77 events (76 robot) against 1 real post, so a broken real event read ok whenever a robot fired it. FIX: scripts/lib/analyticsFreshness.mjs counts people's events only (ROBOT_EVENT_SQL, the exact complement of analytics_events_real) and shows robots as test; scripts/slo.mjs page loads take the same filter (168 before / 168 after: no robot page_load rows today). GUARD: src/test/analyticsCountsReadRealEvents.test.ts (every raw analytics_events read carries the filter or is listed with why; 3 @mutate, all killed). To tick: the next analytics-freshness run on main reports job_posted events = real posts (1 of 77 on 2026-10-21).TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, near done: the corrected 10-21 measurement says the only leak was the analytics freshness monitor; the fix and its reader guard are on ready/builder1i-shell-2 @ 5ae32a4653 in TRAIN B, not yet on origin/main. Tick when TRAIN B lands.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, near done: the corrected 10-21 measurement says the only leak was the analytics freshness monitor; the fix and its reader guard are on ready/builder1i-shell-2 @ 5ae32a4653 in TRAIN B, not yet on origin/main. Tick when TRAIN B lands.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Land the analytics freshness-monitor fix (ready/builder1i-shell-2) or confirm pre-train-1030c's test-data-never-counts change covers it.

- [ ] **Q2148 MEDIUM Two refund paths still return the tax on a kept amount (jobs-fee lane, 2026-10-18; follow-up of Q2082 (a), owner 'J3': the tax on a kept amount is kept from the refund).** (1) process-scheduled-payouts' crew unfilled-spot refund keeps the unfilled share's card fee (flatCardFeeKeptCents, and on a legacy job the fee share crewFeeShareReturnCents holds back) but returns the whole unfilled share's tax (taxReturnCents), so the tax on that kept card fee stays collected; it prices with crewFeeShareReturnCents, not refundToPayerCents, so src/test/refundKeepsTaxOnKeptAmounts.test.ts's inventory does not see it. Fix: subtract taxOnKeptCents({ keptCents: cardFeeKeptCents, taxCents, capturedCents }) from owedCents and from owedAtFee, keeping the Q1827 re-run parity. (2) void-cancelled-payments' late-cancel HOLD capture (captures only the cancellation fee and releases the rest) keeps the fee with no tax on it: read whether Stripe Tax records a partially captured Checkout charge on the captured amount before changing it. (3) The post-cancel toast (src/lib/cancelRefundCopy.ts, 'less the card-processing fee Stripe keeps') does not mention the kept tax on a taxed job (lh-money-escrow review of 461a6b724 #7). (4) Shops and Declutter: shop-checkout collects tax (stripe-webhook/handlers/shopCheckout.ts) and declutter-money refunds by SQL-decided refund_cents; check whether any of their refunds keeps a fee on a taxed order and, if so, apply the same rule (review #8). (5) charge-recurring-visits' stale-visit sweep (the refundToPayerCents call with taxBaseCents = row.amount_cents - row.fee_cents) leaves a POSTER-FEE visit's taxed fee line out of the base, so it keeps no tax on the card fee there (the mid-run path includes it); join the parent's poster_fee_flat_cents or store the tax transaction's total on the row (lh-money-escrow delta review of 2e397597d #1; 0 visit PaymentIntents on prod, switch off). (6) Not measured: whether the restricted/live Stripe key of void-cancelled-payments, execute-dispute-split and money-reconciliation may read Checkout Sessions; a refused read keeps no tax with only a console.warn (delta review #3). GUARD when built: the crew case in src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts*.test.ts with a taxed job asserting the refund is 1 tax-on-kept less, and the inventory extended to crewFeeShareReturnCents callers. finding: lh-money-escrow@461a6b724#8 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, filed from 2f74e262ba; the two refund paths are not fixed.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **STATUS 2026-11-02 (Builder 15, read-only):** (1) and (5) are on main (8879135a7d, lead PASS). (3) superseded (owner 10-25 no fee words; the toast says 'most of'). (4) not real: declutter-checkout refuses any order with tax_cents <> 0 (no tax line before the tax slice; prod 2026-11-02: 0 of 1 declutter_orders taxed), and Shops already keep the tax on a kept card fee (shop_refunds_tax_kept / shop_refunds_amount CHECKs, 20261019071055; 0 shop_refunds rows). Left: (2) and (6), which need a Stripe TEST key to measure (none on this Mac).

- [~] **Q2277 LOW Q2273 follow-ups: a money-owed deletion block can outlast the member's control.** (a) DECIDED (owner 2026-10-21): KEEP BLOCKING, no forfeit state, won't build. Was: a cancellation-fee row 'failed' for "no payout account" blocks until the Helpr connects payouts, and a held tip stays 'owed'/'reversed' while the Helpr is on a payout hold; neither ledger has a forfeit state, so a held or banned Helpr's account cannot be deleted (by them or an admin) without a hand edit. Needs an owner decision: a 'forfeited' status + an admin action (with an audit row), or keep blocking. (b) The crew chargeback read blocks every roster member while the job is 'chargeback', including one already re-paid or removed; narrow it to the member's own unpaid share (chargeback_clawbacks per helper). (c) Fail-closed trade-off: a 'chargeback' job blocks unless dispute_status is 'dispute_lost', but a card dispute LOST on a job that already carried an internal dispute status keeps that status, so its Helpr stays blocked until someone clears it; a card-dispute outcome column (written by chargeDisputeCreated/Closed, never overwritten by internal statuses) would end it. (d) chargeback_clawbacks 'repay_failed' has no automatic exit: the repay_failed alert should tell ops to mark the row 'repaid' with the transfer id after paying by hand, as the tip re-drive alert does. Done when: (a) is decided (keep blocking), (b) a test of a re-paid crew member not blocked, red on today's read, (c) a card-dispute outcome column with a test that a job lost on top of an internal status does not block, (d) the repay_failed alert names the mark-repaid step (alert copy test). finding: lh-money-escrow@dbfe9ca9c0#5 **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Parts (b) re-paid crew not blocked, (c) card-dispute outcome column and (d) repay_failed alert naming are still to build.

- [ ] **Q2444 MEDIUM A held card for a far-out job of $300 or more (or a taxed category) drops off the listing about every 6.5 days (Q2399 round-2 money review #2; finding: lh-money-escrow@b0efb428d3#2).** Such a job is held at posting (it cannot be held off-session later); the 7-day authorization reaches the renewal window, offSessionAllowed refuses an off-session renewal (Q210b / taxed), and failHold sets payment_status 'failed', which unlists the job and asks the poster to pay again. A job posted 3 weeks out cycles 2-3 times. Fix to decide: ask the poster to renew on a Checkout page BEFORE the hold lapses while keeping the job listed, or charge such jobs at posting (today's escrow). Owner question filed 2026-10-26: issue #3658 (owner-needed). Done when: a $300+ job 3 weeks out stays listed until its hire with at most one poster action. done-when: test src/test/edge/card-hold.test.ts **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [ ] **Q2445 MEDIUM The admin refund and Quick Refund success legs of journeys 04-admin-safety and 04-money-outcomes no longer run (Q2399 round-2 silent-failure review #6; finding: lh-silent-failure@b0efb428d3#6).** Once card_hold_at_post is on for the poster (test accounts or everyone; it lands off, 2026-10-27) an open job never holds captured money, so both legs assert the refusal on a held card and then skip as justified (e2e/skipAllowlist.ts). A refund of captured money needs a HIRED job: add a journey that hires on the held card (capture_hold), then drives the admin refund UI and Quick Refund on that captured charge (Stripe test mode, test accounts). Done when: both refund legs run green on a hired job and their skip entries are removed. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.**
- [~] **Q2472 MEDIUM Declutter: "Keep it up until it sells" and seller-picked porch days (owner 2026-10-29, pop-up answers; builder 23).** OWNER DECIDED: (1) a per-listing seller switch "Keep it up until it sells", OFF by default; when on, the 30-day round renews with no limit and no "ends soon" notices; off behaves as Q2461's 30-day rounds. (2) Porch pickup: the seller ticks the WEEKDAYS they can set the item out (separate from in-person pickup times); the buyer picks one of those days in the next 7. Done when both are live for everyone who can see Declutter. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: partly done.** Left: Seller-picked porch weekdays (buyer picks one in the next 7) remain; related work on wip/b44-export-porch-days, wip/b46-porch-places.
- [ ] **Q2471 MEDIUM Owner's ease-of-use list (owner 2026-10-29, pop-up answers): start after the Declutter features land.** (1) Post a job from a photo or voice: Claude writes the whole post (title, details, category, time, suggested price). (2) Declutter buyer offer helper: "Make an offer" shows Claude's suggested amount. (3) One-tap "Book <Helpr> again" from a finished job: copy, direct-offer, pick a date. (4) Plain-words "How does this work?" help on money screens, answered by Claude from our own rules only. Also added to builder 26: Declutter sellers can choose "No offers: this is my price" (take it or leave it). **(3) BUILT 2026-10-29 (builder 49, branch wip/b49-book-again):** "Book <First> again" on the finished job (Posts Completed step, under More; Job done screen beside Hire again) opens /post-job?rebook=<job>&offerTo=<helper>&bookAgain=1: the job copied (never its date) as a direct offer to the same Helpr, opening at When and where (6 of 7). Hire again stays the blank post (owner 2026-10-25); the Job done row also gets the save heart. GUARD: src/test/bookAgainOneTap.test.tsx (8 of 8 @mutate red). **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **(1) PHOTO BUILT 2026-11-04 (Builder 92, ready/b92-ai-photo-post):** the AI job builder on Post a job takes one photo ("Add a photo") with or without words; ai-job-builder checks it by its bytes (JPEG/PNG/WebP, 1.5 MB cap) and Claude writes the whole post. GUARD: src/test/edge/aiJobBuilderPhoto.test.tsx (6/6 @mutate killed). Voice left: phone keyboard dictation fills the box today; a mic button is a follow-up. **(1) VOICE BUILT 2026-11-04 (Builder 93, ready/b93-ai-voice, stacked on ready/b92-ai-photo-post):** the AI job builder gets a "Say it" pill beside "Add a photo", using the chat composer's own dictation (useVoiceDictation, Web Speech API; the iOS app already carries NSSpeechRecognitionUsageDescription, added 2026-08-19 for the chat mic). Each finished phrase is added to the typed words; errors say why (mic off, speech off, offline). The pill shows only where the API exists; where it does not (a WebView without it), the phone keyboard's own mic dictates into the same box, which the lead counts as done. GUARD: src/test/aiJobBuilderVoice.test.tsx (red 2/4 on the pre-voice file; vacuity 3/3). Not measured on a device (no device here). **(2) and (4) BUILT 2026-11-04 (Builder 109, ready/b109-finish):** (2) Make an offer shows Claude's suggested amount: declutter-ai-reply offer mode (someone else's LIVE item that takes offers; the seller's lowest price is never read; whole dollars from half the price, rounded up, to a dollar under it; our own sold range via declutter_price_hint; 30 an hour after the gates) and OfferSuggestion in the buyer's open offer box (one tap fills the amount; a failed suggestion shows nothing); the Privacy AI line names it. GUARD src/test/declutterOfferSuggestion.test.tsx (6 @mutate, each killed). (4) "How does this work?" on the money tab (MoneyHelp, profile earnings/payment tab): support-ai-answer's ask mode, answered from the Help Center only (no tools, no account data, action promises dropped there), no answer points to Support. GUARD src/test/moneyHelp.test.tsx (3 @mutate). Not shot in a browser (one-agent browser rule; sign-in needed); edge deploy owed for (2).

- [~] **Q2585 MEDIUM No Declutter stage preview exists, so audits cannot walk the order stages (Auditor 1 Declutter audit 1102a, 11-02, local build of origin/main 7138fd53ca on the prod backend, read only; ~/.lh-shots/audit-1102a-findings.md, screenshots in ~/.lh-shots/audit-1102a/).** Prod has one Declutter order ever and the test accounts own no live items, offers, wanted posts or orders (Auditor 1, SELECT 2:40 AM 11-02), so paid, booked, on the way, handed over, refund, problem, haul-away and donation cannot be walked read-only. Build an admin-only Declutter preview like the job-page stage preview (mock data, every order stage, both sides). **BUILT 2026-11-02 (Builder 169, wip/b169-open):** /admin/preview/declutter (ProtectedRoute + AdminRoute, its own lazy chunk) draws the REAL Buying view (BuyingDash) or Selling view (SellingDash + My listings) on made-up samples. There are 14 stages: in the cart, paid, booked, porch, porch set out, safe meeting spot, seller delivers, Helpr delivery, handed over, refunded, problem, ran out, haul-away, donation. Each can be shown as the buyer or the seller. The address picks the stage and side (?stage=booked&side=seller), so an audit opens any of them at 375 or 1440 directly, and a Phone window button opens a 375-wide window. The fetch layer allows only what is listed and blocks the rest (src/pages/admin/preview/declutterPreviewBackend.ts). Only sign-in traffic and the admin's own profile and role read leave the browser. Seven listed reads come from the sample. Every other RPC, every table write, function and storage call is answered without being sent. It is linked from Admin > Settings ("Declutter stage preview") and is a catalog row (admin-preview-declutter). Guards: src/test/adminDeclutterPreviewIsInert.test.ts (all 495 RPC names the app source calls, measured 11-02, sent through it, none reaches the network; 6 @mutate killed) and src/pages/admin/preview/declutterPreviewPage.test.tsx (all 28 stage and side pairs render the real views with no Supabase request; 2 @mutate killed). Left: (1) a look at every stage and side at 375 and 1440 (needs a look). Reads not on the list (pickup windows, meetup spot, order chat, Helpr task view, refund view) answer empty, so some stage details may draw thinner than the real thing; add each to the list as an audit finds it. (2) The item page and checkout are not in the preview yet. done-when: test src/test/adminDeclutterPreviewIsInert.test.ts **Audit 1102c P1 + P2 FIXED 2026-11-02 (Builder 172, wip/b172-preview, lead: blockers):** P2: a crash in a view is caught by a boundary INSIDE the preview backend and inert navigation (the controls too), so the fetch layer stays until the route unmounts and the fallback has no link; guard src/pages/admin/preview/declutterPreviewCrash.test.tsx (a thrown view, then reads and a write: zero real fetches; red on the old page). P1: an unlisted read RPC answers null, not []; each stage's sample answers every read and table its views make (free pause, porch days, pickup windows, porch places, meetup spots and the order's spot, seller delivery offer and booking, Helpr task view and raise status, order problem, order place, item address, donation partners, order rows); an unlisted one is reported to declutterPreviewChecks.onUnlisted and fails src/pages/admin/preview/declutterPreviewPage.test.tsx (red with the old samples: 138 unlisted). Left: P3-P7 and R1-R14 (filed by the lead).
